# Complete-line placement, preserving paragraph indentation and ordinary spaces.
nfp78_add_line = {
 set_variable = { name = nfp119_next value = var:nfp78_row }
 change_variable = { name = nfp119_next add = $height$ }
 set_variable = { name = nfp119_limit value = 15 }
 if = { limit = { var:nfp78_sheets > 1 } set_variable = { name = nfp119_limit value = 21 } }
 if = { limit = { var:nfp78_row > 0 var:nfp119_next > var:nfp119_limit }
  change_variable = { name = nfp78_column add = 1 }
  if = { limit = { var:nfp78_column = 3 }
   set_variable = { name = nfp78_column value = 1 }
   change_variable = { name = nfp78_sheets add = 1 }
  }
  set_variable = { name = nfp78_row value = 0 }
 }
 create_container = { tags = { nfp78_line } parent = scope:nfp78_article save_scope_as = nfp78_line }
 scope:nfp78_line = {
  set_variable = { name = nfp78_article value = scope:nfp78_article }
  set_variable = { name = nfp78_sheet value = scope:nfp78_article.var:nfp78_sheets }
  set_variable = { name = nfp78_column value = scope:nfp78_article.var:nfp78_column }
  set_variable = { name = nfp78_key value = flag:$key$ }
 }
 add_to_variable_list = { name = nfp78_lines target = scope:nfp78_line }
 change_variable = { name = nfp78_row add = $height$ }
}

nfp78_append_block = {
 if = { limit = { var:$field$ = flag:NFP63_CLOSING }
  set_variable = { name = nfp78_wrapper value = flag:NFP63_EDITORIAL }
  nfp78_append_key = { field = nfp78_wrapper }
  nfp78_append_key = { field = nfp63_closing }
 } else_if = { limit = { var:$field$ = flag:NFP50_FAMILY_BODY }
  nfp78_append_key = { field = nfp30_body }
